#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=frontend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc api-backend -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc api-backend -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc api-backend -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=notification-service -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc message-broker -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc message-broker -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc message-broker -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')

SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc auth-service -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

EXIT_CODE=""

    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break

  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"

done

  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"

--- TEST SCRIPT ---
#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=auth-service -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  # Invert success condition for DENY test: success means connection was blocked (non-zero exit)
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection was denied as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection was not denied as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

--- TEST OUTPUT ---
=== TEST START ===
SUCCESS: Connection was denied as expected.

======================================================================
TIMESTAMP: 2026-07-11T14:20:39+02:00
OUTCOME:   PASS
======================================================================

{"prompt":"Deny all ingress to payment-service on port 3000 in test-pods."}

--- GENERATED YAML ---
apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: deny-all-ingress-to-payment-service
  namespace: test-pods
spec:
  endpointSelector:
    matchLabels:
      app: payment-service
  ingressDeny:
  - fromEndpoints:
    - matchLabels: {}

--- TEST SCRIPT ---
#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=auth-service -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc payment-service -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc payment-service -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc payment-service -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  # Invert SUCCESS_COND because we expect DENIED (non-zero exit)
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection was denied as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

--- TEST OUTPUT ---
=== TEST START ===
SUCCESS: Connection was denied as expected.

======================================================================
TIMESTAMP: 2026-07-11T21:03:47+02:00
OUTCOME:   PASS
======================================================================

#!/bin/bash

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""

  EXIT_CODE=$?

    RESULT=1
    break

  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=frontend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc api-backend -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""

  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  # Invert success condition for DENY test: success means connection was *blocked*
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection was denied as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection was not denied as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  # For DENIED expected result, SUCCESS_COND is when connection fails (non-zero exit code)
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection was denied as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

{"prompt":"Allow egress from api-backend to cache on TCP port 6379 in test-pods."}

--- GENERATED YAML ---
[document 1/2]
apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: allow-api-backend-to-cache
  namespace: test-pods
spec:
  endpointSelector:
    matchLabels:
      app: api-backend
  egress:
  - toEndpoints:
    - matchLabels:
        app: cache
    toPorts:
    - ports:
      - port: '6379'
        protocol: TCP
---
[document 2/2]
apiVersion: cilium.io/v2
kind: CiliumNetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: allow-cache-from-api-backend
  namespace: test-pods
spec:
  endpointSelector:
    matchLabels:
      app: cache
  ingress:
  - fromEndpoints:
    - matchLabels:
        app: api-backend
    toPorts:
    - ports:
      - port: '6379'
        protocol: TCP

--- TEST SCRIPT ---
#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

--- TEST OUTPUT ---
=== TEST START ===
SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected.

======================================================================
TIMESTAMP: 2026-07-13T18:29:30+02:00
OUTCOME:   PASS

SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc grpc-service -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

RESULT=0

EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )

    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."

done

  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1

exit 0

TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc message-broker -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc message-broker -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc message-broker -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?

  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi

done

  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=frontend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc notification-service -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc notification-service -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc notification-service -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection succeeded as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not succeed."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

[document 1/2]
apiVersion: crd.projectcalico.org/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: allow-api-backend-to-cache
  namespace: test-pods
spec:
  selector: app == "api-backend"
  egress:
  - action: Allow
    protocol: TCP
    destination:
      selector: app == "cache"
      ports:
      - 6379
---
[document 2/2]
apiVersion: crd.projectcalico.org/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: allow-cache-from-api-backend
  namespace: test-pods
spec:
  selector: app == "cache"
  ingress:
  - action: Allow
    protocol: TCP
    source:
      selector: app == "api-backend"
    destination:
      ports:
      - 6379

--- TEST SCRIPT ---
#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

--- TEST OUTPUT ---
=== TEST START ===
SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected.

======================================================================
TIMESTAMP: 2026-07-17T19:59:29+02:00
OUTCOME:   PASS
======================================================================

set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

--- INTENT ---
{"prompt":"Block all egress from frontend to api-backend on TCP port 8000 in test-pods."}

--- GENERATED YAML ---
apiVersion: crd.projectcalico.org/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: deny-frontend-to-api-backend
  namespace: test-pods
spec:
  selector: app == "frontend"
  egress:
  - action: Deny
    protocol: TCP
    destination:
      selector: app == "api-backend"
      ports:
      - 8000
  - action: Allow
    destination:
      selector: all()

--- TEST SCRIPT ---
#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=frontend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc api-backend -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc api-backend -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=8000

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection denied as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection was allowed but should be denied."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

--- TEST OUTPUT ---
=== TEST START ===
SUCCESS: Connection denied as expected.

======================================================================
TIMESTAMP: 2026-07-18T09:41:25+02:00
OUTCOME:   PASS
======================================================================

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')

SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  # EXPECTED: denied → connection should FAIL (non-zero exit)
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection denied as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i: connection succeeded (unexpected). Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection was allowed but should have been denied."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=api-backend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc message-broker -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc message-broker -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc message-broker -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -eq 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection behaved as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i failed. Tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection did not behave as expected."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

--- TEST SCRIPT ---
#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=grpc-service -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc payment-service -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }
SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc payment-service -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc payment-service -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  # For DENIED expectation: nc -z fails (exit != 0) when connection is blocked
  # So successful block = EXIT_CODE != 0 (connection refused/timeout)
  if [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection denied as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i: Connection was allowed (unexpected)."
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection was allowed when it should have been denied."

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=frontend -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

kubectl get svc api-backend -n $TARGET_NS >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo "SKIP: target service not found in ns $TARGET_NS"; exit 0; }

SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc api-backend -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- nc -z -w 3 $SVC_IP $SVC_PORT 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  # For DENY intent: connection should FAIL (exit code != 0)
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection denied as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i: Connection was allowed (unexpected)"
  sleep 2
done

if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection was allowed (should have been denied)."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

SOURCE_POD=$(kubectl get pods -n $SOURCE_NS -l app=payment-service -o jsonpath='{.items[0].metadata.name}')
if [ -z "$SOURCE_POD" ]; then echo "SKIP: source pod not found in ns $SOURCE_NS"; exit 0; fi

SVC_IP=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.clusterIP}')
SVC_PORT=$(kubectl get svc cache -n $TARGET_NS -o jsonpath='{.spec.ports[0].port}')

echo "=== TEST START ==="
RESULT=0
CMD_OUTPUT=""
EXIT_CODE=""
for i in 1 2 3 4 5; do
  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)
  EXIT_CODE=$?
  # BLOCKED CONNECTION TEST: invert logic - connection failure means blocked (SUCCESS)
  SUCCESS_COND=$( [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ] && echo 1 || echo 0 )
  if [ $SUCCESS_COND -eq 1 ]; then
    echo "SUCCESS: Connection blocked as expected."
    RESULT=1
    break
  fi
  echo "Attempt $i: connection succeeded (not blocked)."
  sleep 2
done
if [ $RESULT -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "FAIL: Connection was NOT blocked (expected to be denied)."
  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
  exit 1
fi
exit 0

#!/bin/bash
set -uo pipefail

SOURCE_NS="test-pods"
TARGET_NS="test-pods"

EXIT_CODE=""

  CMD_OUTPUT=$(kubectl exec -n $SOURCE_NS $SOURCE_POD -- timeout 3 bash -c "echo > /dev/tcp/$SVC_IP/$SVC_PORT" 2>&1)

  echo "Last tool output: $CMD_OUTPUT"
  echo "Last exit code: $EXIT_CODE"
